  },
  "cells": [
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# Change these to the actual paths in your Colab filesystem\n",
        "TRAIN_PATH = \"/content/PenDigits_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "TEST_PATH  = \"/content/PenDigits_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 64\n",
        "EPOCHS     = 40\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "DROPOUT    = 0.2\n",
        "\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 64      # channels in TCN\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim in temporal GCN branch\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim used inside Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = 4 # number of segments for Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Utilities\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Convert sktime panel DataFrame (each cell is a pd.Series)\n",
        "    to numpy array of shape (n_samples, seq_len, n_dims)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    # assume all series same length\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        X: (N, T, C)\n",
        "        y: (N,)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Build normalized adjacency for a chain graph over T nodes:\n",
        "      0-1-2-...-(T-1), plus self loops.\n",
        "    Returns A_norm: [T, T]\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T, device=device)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1, device=device)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)  # [T]\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "    return A_norm\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Load PenDigits TS data\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Loading PenDigits train/test from TS files...\")\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TRAIN_PATH)\n",
        "X_test_df,  y_test_raw  = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TEST_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to numpy (N, T, C)\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Encode labels 0..(n_classes-1)\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Number of classes:\", n_classes)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build datasets and loaders\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "print(\"Sequence length:\", seq_len, \"| Num features:\", n_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Core Layers (TCN, GCN, GOG)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2 * dilation  # \"same\" padding\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.downsample = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1) \\\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C_in, T]\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        channels = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(\n",
        "                    in_channels=channels,\n",
        "                    out_channels=hidden_channels,\n",
        "                    kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                    dilation=dilation,\n",
        "                    dropout=dropout\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            channels = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C, T]\n",
        "        return self.network(x)  # [B, hidden_channels, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph Convolution blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        A: [N, N] (normalized adjacency)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)  # [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)                   # [B, N, F_out]\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A simple stack of GraphConv layers, with optional input projection.\n",
        "    Used both for inner (graph) and outer (graph-of-graphs) levels.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.use_proj = (in_dim != hidden_dim)\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in or hidden_dim]\n",
        "        A: [N, N]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            h = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            h = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h  # [B, N, hidden_dim]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GOG: Graph-of-Graphs block ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Graph-of-Graphs for sequences:\n",
        "\n",
        "    1) Split sequence over time into S segments -> each segment is a \"graph\".\n",
        "    2) For each segment:\n",
        "       - build a chain graph over its time steps\n",
        "       - run inner GCN and pool to get one embedding per segment\n",
        "    3) Now treat segment embeddings as nodes of a higher-level graph (outer graph):\n",
        "       - build chain graph over segments\n",
        "       - run outer GCN to get refined segment embeddings\n",
        "    4) Global pooling over segments -> final GOG representation.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_dim,\n",
        "                 hidden_dim,\n",
        "                 num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1,\n",
        "                 outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, F_in]\n",
        "        returns: [B, hidden_dim]  (GOG representation)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Split time dimension into segments (graphs)\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, chunks=self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list of [B, T_seg, F]\n",
        "\n",
        "        seg_emb_list = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)          # [T_seg, T_seg]\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)                  # [B, T_seg, H]\n",
        "            seg_emb = h_inner.mean(dim=1)                           # [B, H] (graph pooling)\n",
        "            seg_emb_list.append(seg_emb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Stack segment embeddings -> graph-of-graphs nodes\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_emb_list, dim=1)  # [B, S, H], S=num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Outer graph adjacency over segments (chain)\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)  # [S, S]\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)                # [B, S, H]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Global pooling over GOG nodes\n",
        "        gog_repr = h_outer.mean(dim=1)                              # [B, H]\n",
        "        return gog_repr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.0,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # TCN over time (channels = features)\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        # Graph-of-Graphs on TCN features over time\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        # TCN wants [B, C, T]\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)      # [B, C, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = self.tcn(x_tcn)      # [B, tcn_hidden, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = tcn_out.transpose(1, 2)  # [B, T, tcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(tcn_out)    # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "            for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal chain graph for all time steps\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)  # [T, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)                     # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)                  # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(h)                     # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Combined model: TCN-GOG + GCN-GOG ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            tcn_hidden=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=3,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            gcn_hidden=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Each branch outputs gog_hidden, so combined is 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 128),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        tcn_gog_out = self.tcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        gcn_gog_out = self.gcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_gog_out, gcn_gog_out], dim=-1)  # [B, 2*gog_hidden]\n",
        "        logits = self.classifier(combined)                        # [B, n_classes]\n",
        "        return logits\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Training & Evaluation\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 7. Instantiate & Train (on T4 GPU)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state_dict = None\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device)\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc   = evaluate(model, test_loader,  criterion, device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state_dict = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best Test Acc={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}%\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state_dict is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state_dict)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# Change these to the actual paths in your Colab filesystem\n",
        "TRAIN_PATH = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "TEST_PATH  = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 128\n",
        "EPOCHS     = 40\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "DROPOUT    = 0.2\n",
        "\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 64      # channels in TCN\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim in temporal GCN branch\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim used inside Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = 4 # number of segments for Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Utilities\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Convert sktime panel DataFrame (each cell is a pd.Series)\n",
        "    to numpy array of shape (n_samples, seq_len, n_dims)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    # assume all series same length\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        X: (N, T, C)\n",
        "        y: (N,)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Build normalized adjacency for a chain graph over T nodes:\n",
        "      0-1-2-...-(T-1), plus self loops.\n",
        "    Returns A_norm: [T, T]\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T, device=device)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1, device=device)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)  # [T]\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "    return A_norm\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Load PenDigits TS data\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Loading PenDigits train/test from TS files...\")\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TRAIN_PATH)\n",
        "X_test_df,  y_test_raw  = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TEST_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to numpy (N, T, C)\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Encode labels 0..(n_classes-1)\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Number of classes:\", n_classes)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build datasets and loaders\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "print(\"Sequence length:\", seq_len, \"| Num features:\", n_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Core Layers (TCN, GCN, GOG)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2 * dilation  # \"same\" padding\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.downsample = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1) \\\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C_in, T]\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        channels = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(\n",
        "                    in_channels=channels,\n",
        "                    out_channels=hidden_channels,\n",
        "                    kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                    dilation=dilation,\n",
        "                    dropout=dropout\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            channels = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C, T]\n",
        "        return self.network(x)  # [B, hidden_channels, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph Convolution blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        A: [N, N] (normalized adjacency)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)  # [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)                   # [B, N, F_out]\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A simple stack of GraphConv layers, with optional input projection.\n",
        "    Used both for inner (graph) and outer (graph-of-graphs) levels.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.use_proj = (in_dim != hidden_dim)\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in or hidden_dim]\n",
        "        A: [N, N]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            h = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            h = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h  # [B, N, hidden_dim]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GOG: Graph-of-Graphs block ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Graph-of-Graphs for sequences:\n",
        "\n",
        "    1) Split sequence over time into S segments -> each segment is a \"graph\".\n",
        "    2) For each segment:\n",
        "       - build a chain graph over its time steps\n",
        "       - run inner GCN and pool to get one embedding per segment\n",
        "    3) Now treat segment embeddings as nodes of a higher-level graph (outer graph):\n",
        "       - build chain graph over segments\n",
        "       - run outer GCN to get refined segment embeddings\n",
        "    4) Global pooling over segments -> final GOG representation.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_dim,\n",
        "                 hidden_dim,\n",
        "                 num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1,\n",
        "                 outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, F_in]\n",
        "        returns: [B, hidden_dim]  (GOG representation)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Split time dimension into segments (graphs)\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, chunks=self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list of [B, T_seg, F]\n",
        "\n",
        "        seg_emb_list = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)          # [T_seg, T_seg]\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)                  # [B, T_seg, H]\n",
        "            seg_emb = h_inner.mean(dim=1)                           # [B, H] (graph pooling)\n",
        "            seg_emb_list.append(seg_emb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Stack segment embeddings -> graph-of-graphs nodes\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_emb_list, dim=1)  # [B, S, H], S=num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Outer graph adjacency over segments (chain)\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)  # [S, S]\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)                # [B, S, H]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Global pooling over GOG nodes\n",
        "        gog_repr = h_outer.mean(dim=1)                              # [B, H]\n",
        "        return gog_repr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.0,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # TCN over time (channels = features)\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        # Graph-of-Graphs on TCN features over time\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        # TCN wants [B, C, T]\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)      # [B, C, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = self.tcn(x_tcn)      # [B, tcn_hidden, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = tcn_out.transpose(1, 2)  # [B, T, tcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(tcn_out)    # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "            for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal chain graph for all time steps\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)  # [T, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)                     # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)                  # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(h)                     # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Combined model: TCN-GOG + GCN-GOG ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            tcn_hidden=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=3,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            gcn_hidden=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Each branch outputs gog_hidden, so combined is 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 128),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        tcn_gog_out = self.tcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        gcn_gog_out = self.gcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_gog_out, gcn_gog_out], dim=-1)  # [B, 2*gog_hidden]\n",
        "        logits = self.classifier(combined)                        # [B, n_classes]\n",
        "        return logits\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Training & Evaluation\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 7. Instantiate & Train (on T4 GPU)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state_dict = None\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device)\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc   = evaluate(model, test_loader,  criterion, device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state_dict = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best Test Acc={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}%\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state_dict is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state_dict)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "7UwVogEXY1mN"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# Change these to the actual paths in your Colab filesystem\n",
        "TRAIN_PATH = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "TEST_PATH  = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 128\n",
        "EPOCHS     = 40\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "DROPOUT    = 0.2\n",
        "\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 64      # channels in TCN\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim in temporal GCN branch\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 64      # hidden dim used inside Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = 4 # number of segments for Graph-of-Graphs\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_ts_mts(path):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Universal loader for UCR/UEA .ts multivariate datasets.\n",
        "    Pads or truncates all channels to the max sequence length found.\n",
        "    Returns: X (N,T,C), y (N,)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    X_df, y = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    N, C = X_df.shape\n",
        "    # find max length across all series / channels\n",
        "    max_len = max(X_df.iloc[i, j].size for i in range(N) for j in range(C))\n",
        "\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N, max_len, C), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            s = X_df.iloc[i, j].astype(np.float32).to_numpy()\n",
        "            L = len(s)\n",
        "            if L >= max_len:\n",
        "                X[i, :, j] = s[:max_len]\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                X[i, :L, j] = s           # resting padded with zeros\n",
        "\n",
        "    return X, np.array(y)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Utilities\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Convert sktime panel DataFrame (each cell is a pd.Series)\n",
        "    to numpy array of shape (n_samples, seq_len, n_dims)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    # assume all series same length\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        X: (N, T, C)\n",
        "        y: (N,)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Build normalized adjacency for a chain graph over T nodes:\n",
        "      0-1-2-...-(T-1), plus self loops.\n",
        "    Returns A_norm: [T, T]\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T, device=device)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1, device=device)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)  # [T]\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "    return A_norm\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Load PenDigits TS data\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Loading PenDigits train/test from TS files...\")\n",
        "# X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TRAIN_PATH)\n",
        "# X_test_df,  y_test_raw  = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(TEST_PATH)\n",
        "X_train, y_train_raw = load_ts_mts(TRAIN_PATH)\n",
        "X_test,  y_test_raw  = load_ts_mts(TEST_PATH)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to numpy (N, T, C)\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Encode labels 0..(n_classes-1)\n",
        "# le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "# y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "# y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "# n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "# print(\"Number of classes:\", n_classes)\n",
        "# print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_))\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Number of classes:\", n_classes)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build datasets and loaders\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "print(\"Sequence length:\", seq_len, \"| Num features:\", n_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Core Layers (TCN, GCN, GOG)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2 * dilation  # \"same\" padding\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.downsample = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1) \\\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C_in, T]\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        channels = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(\n",
        "                    in_channels=channels,\n",
        "                    out_channels=hidden_channels,\n",
        "                    kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                    dilation=dilation,\n",
        "                    dropout=dropout\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            channels = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C, T]\n",
        "        return self.network(x)  # [B, hidden_channels, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph Convolution blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        A: [N, N] (normalized adjacency)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)  # [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)                   # [B, N, F_out]\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A simple stack of GraphConv layers, with optional input projection.\n",
        "    Used both for inner (graph) and outer (graph-of-graphs) levels.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.use_proj = (in_dim != hidden_dim)\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in or hidden_dim]\n",
        "        A: [N, N]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            h = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            h = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h  # [B, N, hidden_dim]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GOG: Graph-of-Graphs block ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Graph-of-Graphs for sequences:\n",
        "\n",
        "    1) Split sequence over time into S segments -> each segment is a \"graph\".\n",
        "    2) For each segment:\n",
        "       - build a chain graph over its time steps\n",
        "       - run inner GCN and pool to get one embedding per segment\n",
        "    3) Now treat segment embeddings as nodes of a higher-level graph (outer graph):\n",
        "       - build chain graph over segments\n",
        "       - run outer GCN to get refined segment embeddings\n",
        "    4) Global pooling over segments -> final GOG representation.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_dim,\n",
        "                 hidden_dim,\n",
        "                 num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1,\n",
        "                 outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, F_in]\n",
        "        returns: [B, hidden_dim]  (GOG representation)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Split time dimension into segments (graphs)\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, chunks=self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list of [B, T_seg, F]\n",
        "\n",
        "        seg_emb_list = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)          # [T_seg, T_seg]\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)                  # [B, T_seg, H]\n",
        "            seg_emb = h_inner.mean(dim=1)                           # [B, H] (graph pooling)\n",
        "            seg_emb_list.append(seg_emb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Stack segment embeddings -> graph-of-graphs nodes\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_emb_list, dim=1)  # [B, S, H], S=num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Outer graph adjacency over segments (chain)\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)  # [S, S]\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)                # [B, S, H]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Global pooling over GOG nodes\n",
        "        gog_repr = h_outer.mean(dim=1)                              # [B, H]\n",
        "        return gog_repr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.0,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # TCN over time (channels = features)\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        # Graph-of-Graphs on TCN features over time\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        # TCN wants [B, C, T]\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)      # [B, C, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = self.tcn(x_tcn)      # [B, tcn_hidden, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = tcn_out.transpose(1, 2)  # [B, T, tcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(tcn_out)    # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "            for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal chain graph for all time steps\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)  # [T, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)                     # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)                  # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(h)                     # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Combined model: TCN-GOG + GCN-GOG ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            tcn_hidden=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=3,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            gcn_hidden=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Each branch outputs gog_hidden, so combined is 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 128),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        tcn_gog_out = self.tcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        gcn_gog_out = self.gcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_gog_out, gcn_gog_out], dim=-1)  # [B, 2*gog_hidden]\n",
        "        logits = self.classifier(combined)                        # [B, n_classes]\n",
        "        return logits\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Training & Evaluation\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 7. Instantiate & Train (on T4 GPU)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state_dict = None\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device)\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc   = evaluate(model, test_loader,  criterion, device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state_dict = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best Test Acc={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}%\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state_dict is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state_dict)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")"
      ],
