      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset  # 👈 use this instead\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"PEMS-SF\"  # just change this string for other datasets\n",
        "# Multivariate UCR/UEA dataset names\n",
        "\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"DuckDuckGeese\"              # DDG\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"FaceDetection\"              # FD\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"FingerMovements\"            # FM\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"Heartbeat\"                  # HB\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"LearningShapes\"             # LS\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"MotorImagery\"               # MI\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"NATOPS\"                     # NA\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"PEMS-SF\"                    # PE\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"PhonemeSpectra\"             # PS\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"SelfRegulationSCP1\"         # SCP1\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"SelfRegulationSCP2\"         # SCP2\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"StandWalkJump\"              # SWJ\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"EigenWorms\"                 # EW\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"ArticularyWordRecognition\"  # AWR\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"EthanolConcentration\"       # EC\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"PenDigits\"                  # PD\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"UWaveGestureLibrary\"        # UWG\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"ERing\"                      # ER\n",
        "# DATASET_NAME = \"JapaneseVowels\"             # JV\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 32\n",
        "EPOCHS     = 100\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "DROPOUT    = 0.2\n",
        "\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = 4\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Utilities\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Convert sktime panel DataFrame (each cell is a pd.Series)\n",
        "    to numpy array of shape (n_samples, seq_len, n_dims)\n",
        "    Assumes all series have the same length.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T, device=device)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1, device=device)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "    return A_norm\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Load ANY UCR/UEA TS dataset (PEMS-SF now)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"Loading {DATASET_NAME} from UCR/UEA archive via sktime...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# sktime will download and parse for you\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"train\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "X_test_df, y_test_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"test\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to numpy (N, T, C)\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Encode labels 0..(n_classes-1)\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Number of classes:\", n_classes)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build datasets and loaders\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "print(\"Sequence length:\", seq_len, \"| Num features:\", n_features)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Core Layers (TCN, GCN, GOG)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2 * dilation  # \"same\" padding\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.downsample = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1) \\\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C_in, T]\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        channels = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(\n",
        "                    in_channels=channels,\n",
        "                    out_channels=hidden_channels,\n",
        "                    kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                    dilation=dilation,\n",
        "                    dropout=dropout\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            channels = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C, T]\n",
        "        return self.network(x)  # [B, hidden_channels, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph Convolution blocks ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        A: [N, N] (normalized adjacency)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)  # [B, N, F_in]\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)                   # [B, N, F_out]\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    A simple stack of GraphConv layers, with optional input projection.\n",
        "    Used both for inner (graph) and outer (graph-of-graphs) levels.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.use_proj = (in_dim != hidden_dim)\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, N, F_in or hidden_dim]\n",
        "        A: [N, N]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        if self.use_proj:\n",
        "            h = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            h = x\n",
        "\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h  # [B, N, hidden_dim]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GOG: Graph-of-Graphs block ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Graph-of-Graphs for sequences:\n",
        "\n",
        "    1) Split sequence over time into S segments -> each segment is a \"graph\".\n",
        "    2) For each segment:\n",
        "       - build a chain graph over its time steps\n",
        "       - run inner GCN and pool to get one embedding per segment\n",
        "    3) Now treat segment embeddings as nodes of a higher-level graph (outer graph):\n",
        "       - build chain graph over segments\n",
        "       - run outer GCN to get refined segment embeddings\n",
        "    4) Global pooling over segments -> final GOG representation.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_dim,\n",
        "                 hidden_dim,\n",
        "                 num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1,\n",
        "                 outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, F_in]\n",
        "        returns: [B, hidden_dim]  (GOG representation)\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Split time dimension into segments (graphs)\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, chunks=self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list of [B, T_seg, F]\n",
        "\n",
        "        seg_emb_list = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)          # [T_seg, T_seg]\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)                  # [B, T_seg, H]\n",
        "            seg_emb = h_inner.mean(dim=1)                           # [B, H] (graph pooling)\n",
        "            seg_emb_list.append(seg_emb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Stack segment embeddings -> graph-of-graphs nodes\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_emb_list, dim=1)  # [B, S, H], S=num_segments\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Outer graph adjacency over segments (chain)\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)  # [S, S]\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)                # [B, S, H]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Global pooling over GOG nodes\n",
        "        gog_repr = h_outer.mean(dim=1)                              # [B, H]\n",
        "        return gog_repr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.0,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # TCN over time (channels = features)\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        # Graph-of-Graphs on TCN features over time\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        # TCN wants [B, C, T]\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)      # [B, C, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = self.tcn(x_tcn)      # [B, tcn_hidden, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = tcn_out.transpose(1, 2)  # [B, T, tcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(tcn_out)    # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- GCN-GOG branch ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "            for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal chain graph for all time steps\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)  # [T, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)                     # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)                  # [B, T, gcn_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        gog_out = self.gog(h)                     # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return gog_out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Combined model: TCN-GOG + GCN-GOG ----------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,\n",
        "                 in_features,\n",
        "                 n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 gog_hidden=64,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "                 gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.2,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            tcn_hidden=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=3,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features=in_features,\n",
        "            gcn_hidden=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            gog_hidden=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Each branch outputs gog_hidden, so combined is 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 128),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        x: [B, T, C]\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        tcn_gog_out = self.tcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "        gcn_gog_out = self.gcn_branch(x)   # [B, gog_hidden]\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_gog_out, gcn_gog_out], dim=-1)  # [B, 2*gog_hidden]\n",
        "        logits = self.classifier(combined)                        # [B, n_classes]\n",
        "        return logits\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Training & Evaluation\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds = []\n",
        "    all_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 7. Instantiate & Train (on T4 GPU)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state_dict = None\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device)\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc   = evaluate(model, test_loader,  criterion, device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state_dict = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best Test Acc={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}%\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state_dict is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state_dict)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "eFVM6MXEdcxe"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "**FASTER VERSION OF ABOVE CODEa**"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "qI5DlQdW5I_Z"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports & Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "\n",
        "# ------------ choose any multivariate dataset here -------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"FaceDetection\"\n",
        "# e.g. \"DuckDuckGeese\", \"FaceDetection\", \"FingerMovements\",\n",
        "#      \"Heartbeat\", \"LearningShapes\", \"MotorImagery\",\n",
        "#      \"NATOPS\", \"PEMS-SF\", \"PhonemeSpectra\", \"SelfRegulationSCP1\",\n",
        "#      \"SelfRegulationSCP2\", \"StandWalkJump\", \"EigenWorms\",\n",
        "#      \"ArticularyWordRecognition\", \"EthanolConcentration\",\n",
        "#      \"PenDigits\", \"UWaveGestureLibrary\", \"ERing\", \"JapaneseVowels\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE   = 64        # a bit smaller for better gradients\n",
        "EPOCHS       = 100       # early stopping will stop earlier\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT      = 0.3\n",
        "\n",
        "# a bit larger than last time for higher capacity\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN   = 64\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN   = 64\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN   = 96\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp_enabled = (device.type == \"cuda\")\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device, \"| AMP:\", amp_enabled)\n",
        "\n",
        "# optional but helpful\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Utilities: data, adjacency, dataset\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Convert sktime panel DataFrame (each cell is a pd.Series)\n",
        "    to numpy array of shape (n_samples, seq_len, n_dims).\n",
        "    Assumes all series have equal length.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "_adj_cache = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Normalized chain adjacency with self-loops, cached by T+device.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    key = (T, device.type)\n",
        "    if key in _adj_cache:\n",
        "        return _adj_cache[key].to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "\n",
        "    _adj_cache[key] = A_norm\n",
        "    return A_norm.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def choose_num_segments(seq_len):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Heuristic for GOG segments based on sequence length.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if seq_len <= 60:\n",
        "        return 3\n",
        "    elif seq_len <= 150:\n",
        "        return 4\n",
        "    elif seq_len <= 300:\n",
        "        return 6\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return 8\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Load + NORMALIZE any UCR/UEA MTS dataset\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"Loading {DATASET_NAME} from UCR/UEA archive...\")\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"train\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "X_test_df, y_test_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"test\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- VERY IMPORTANT: z-normalize per feature using TRAIN stats ----\n",
        "N_train, T, C = X_train.shape\n",
        "N_test = X_test.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train_2d = X_train.reshape(-1, C)      # (N_train*T, C)\n",
        "X_test_2d  = X_test.reshape(-1, C)       # (N_test*T, C)\n",
        "\n",
        "scaler.fit(X_train_2d)\n",
        "X_train = scaler.transform(X_train_2d).reshape(N_train, T, C)\n",
        "X_test  = scaler.transform(X_test_2d).reshape(N_test, T, C)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train shape:\", X_train.shape)\n",
        "print(\"X_test shape :\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_), \"→\", n_classes, \"classes\")\n",
        "\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = choose_num_segments(seq_len)\n",
        "print(\"Seq len:\", seq_len,\n",
        "      \"| Features:\", n_features,\n",
        "      \"| GOG segments:\", NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Core Layers: TCN, GCN, GOG\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- TCN ----------\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2 * dilation\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "        self.downsample = (\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1)\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        channels = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(\n",
        "                    in_channels=channels,\n",
        "                    out_channels=hidden_channels,\n",
        "                    kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                    dilation=dilation,\n",
        "                    dropout=dropout\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            channels = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return self.network(x)   # [B, hidden_channels, T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph Conv ----------\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        # x: [B, N, F], A: [N, N]\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.input_proj = (\n",
        "            nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim) if in_dim != hidden_dim else None\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x) if self.input_proj is not None else x\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Graph-of-Graphs ----------\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1, outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, T, F]\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list of [B, T_seg, F]\n",
        "        seg_embs = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)          # [B, T_seg, H]\n",
        "            seg_embs.append(h_inner.mean(dim=1))            # [B, H]\n",
        "\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_embs, dim=1)            # [B, S, H]\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)        # [B, S, H]\n",
        "        gog_repr = h_outer.mean(dim=1)                      # [B, H]\n",
        "        return gog_repr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Branches ----------\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, tcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3, kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.3, num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)         # [B, C, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = self.tcn(x_tcn)         # [B, tcn_hidden, T]\n",
        "        tcn_out = tcn_out.transpose(1, 2) # [B, T, tcn_hidden]\n",
        "        return self.gog(tcn_out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, gcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2, num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden) for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)\n",
        "        return self.gog(h)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------- Final classifier ----------\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=64, gcn_hidden=64, gog_hidden=96,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3, gcn_layers=2, dropout=0.3,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features, tcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers, kernel_size=3,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout, num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features, gcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2 * gog_hidden\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 192),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(192, 96),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(96, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        tcn_gog = self.tcn_branch(x)\n",
        "        gcn_gog = self.gcn_branch(x)\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_gog, gcn_gog], dim=-1)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(combined)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Training & Evaluation (AMP + early stop)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device, scaler):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds, all_labels = [], []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp_enabled):\n",
        "            logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "            loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if amp_enabled:\n",
        "            scaler.scale(loss).backward()\n",
        "            scaler.step(optimizer)\n",
        "            scaler.update()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            loss.backward()\n",
        "            optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = logits.argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds, all_labels = [], []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp_enabled):\n",
        "            logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "            loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = logits.argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    epoch_loss = running_loss / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "    epoch_acc = accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "    return epoch_loss, epoch_acc\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Train with scheduler + early stopping\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(\n",
        "    model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY\n",
        ")\n",
        "scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(\n",
        "    optimizer, mode=\"max\", factor=0.5, patience=5\n",
        ")\n",
        "scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=amp_enabled)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state = None\n",
        "patience = 15\n",
        "no_improve = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(\n",
        "        model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device, scaler\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc = evaluate(model, test_loader, criterion, device)\n",
        "    old_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"]\n",
        "    scheduler.step(test_acc)\n",
        "    new_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc + 1e-4:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "        no_improve = 0\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        no_improve += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    lr_msg = f\" LR {old_lr:.2e}->{new_lr:.2e}\" if new_lr != old_lr else \"\"\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"NoImp={no_improve}/{patience}{lr_msg}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if no_improve >= patience:\n",
        "        print(\"Early stopping.\")\n",
        "        break\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 1. Imports & Config\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "\n",
        "# ------------ choose any multivariate dataset here -------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"StandWalkJump\"\n",
        "# Examples:\n",
        "# \"DuckDuckGeese\",\"FaceDetection\",\"FingerMovements\",\"Heartbeat\",\n",
        "# \"LSST\",\"MotorImagery\",\"NATOPS\",\"PEMS-SF\",\"PhonemeSpectra\",\n",
        "# \"SelfRegulationSCP1\",\"SelfRegulationSCP2\",\"StandWalkJump\",\"EigenWorms\",\n",
        "# \"ArticularyWordRecognition\",\"EthanolConcentration\",\"PenDigits\",\n",
        "# \"UWaveGestureLibrary\",\"ERing\",\"JapaneseVowels\"\n",
        "\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE   = 32\n",
        "EPOCHS       = 120\n",
        "LEARNING_RATE = 1e-3\n",
        "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT      = 0.3\n",
        "\n",
        "# higher capacity than before\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN   = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN   = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN   = 128\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp_enabled = device.type == \"cuda\"\n",
        "print(\"Using device:\", device, \"| AMP:\", amp_enabled)\n",
        "\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 2. Data utilities\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "def dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    n_samples, n_dims = X_df.shape\n",
        "    seq_len = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((n_samples, seq_len, n_dims), dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(n_samples):\n",
        "        for d in range(n_dims):\n",
        "            X[i, :, d] = X_df.iloc[i, d].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TimeSeriesDataset(Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        self.X = torch.tensor(X, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "        self.y = torch.tensor(y, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __len__(self):\n",
        "        return self.X.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n",
        "        return self.X[idx], self.y[idx]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "_adj_cache = {}\n",
        "def build_chain_adjacency(T, device):\n",
        "    key = (T, device.type)\n",
        "    if key in _adj_cache:\n",
        "        return _adj_cache[key].to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T)\n",
        "    idx = torch.arange(T - 1)\n",
        "    A[idx, idx + 1] = 1.0\n",
        "    A[idx + 1, idx] = 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deg = A.sum(dim=1)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(deg, -0.5)\n",
        "    deg_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(deg_inv_sqrt)] = 0.0\n",
        "    D_inv_sqrt = torch.diag(deg_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "    A_norm = D_inv_sqrt @ A @ D_inv_sqrt\n",
        "    _adj_cache[key] = A_norm\n",
        "    return A_norm.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def choose_num_segments(seq_len):\n",
        "    if seq_len <= 60:\n",
        "        return 3\n",
        "    elif seq_len <= 150:\n",
        "        return 4\n",
        "    elif seq_len <= 300:\n",
        "        return 6\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return 8\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 3. Load + NORMALIZE dataset\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "print(f\"Loading {DATASET_NAME}...\")\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"train\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "X_test_df, y_test_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "    DATASET_NAME, split=\"test\", return_X_y=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = dataframe_to_3d_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "N_train, T, C = X_train.shape\n",
        "N_test = X_test.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "# z-normalize per feature using train stats\n",
        "scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train_2d = X_train.reshape(-1, C)\n",
        "X_test_2d  = X_test.reshape(-1, C)\n",
        "scaler.fit(X_train_2d)\n",
        "X_train = scaler.transform(X_train_2d).reshape(N_train, T, C)\n",
        "X_test  = scaler.transform(X_test_2d).reshape(N_test, T, C)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X_train:\", X_train.shape, \"X_test:\", X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(y_train_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(y_test_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "print(\"Classes:\", list(le.classes_), \"→\", n_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_dataset = TimeSeriesDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_dataset  = TimeSeriesDataset(X_test,  y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=True, drop_last=False)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_dataset,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "                          shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "_, seq_len, n_features = X_train.shape\n",
        "NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS = choose_num_segments(seq_len)\n",
        "print(\"Seq len:\", seq_len, \"| features:\", n_features,\n",
        "      \"| GOG segments:\", NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 4. Core layers (TCN, GCN, GOG)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "class ResidualTCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dilation=1, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        padding = (kernel_size - 1)//2 * dilation\n",
        "        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(out_channels, out_channels, kernel_size,\n",
        "                               padding=padding, dilation=dilation)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "        self.downsample = (\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=1)\n",
        "            if in_channels != out_channels else None\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        residual = x if self.downsample is None else self.downsample(x)\n",
        "        out = self.conv1(x)\n",
        "        out = self.bn1(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "        out = self.conv2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.bn2(out)\n",
        "        out = self.dropout(out)\n",
        "        out = F.relu(out + residual)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels=64, num_layers=3,\n",
        "                 kernel_size=3, dropout=0.0):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        ch = in_channels\n",
        "        for i in range(num_layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2**i\n",
        "            layers.append(\n",
        "                ResidualTCNBlock(ch, hidden_channels,\n",
        "                                 kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "                                 dilation=dilation, dropout=dropout)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            ch = hidden_channels\n",
        "        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):  # x: [B,C,T]\n",
        "        return self.network(x)  # [B,H,T]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_feats, out_feats):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_feats, out_feats)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        # x: [B,N,F], A: [N,N]\n",
        "        Ax = torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)\n",
        "        out = self.linear(Ax)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class SimpleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.input_proj = (\n",
        "            nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim) if in_dim != hidden_dim else None\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList(\n",
        "            [GraphConv(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_layers)]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x) if self.input_proj is not None else x\n",
        "        for layer in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A)\n",
        "        return h  # [B,N,H]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphOfGraphs(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_dim, hidden_dim, num_segments=4,\n",
        "                 inner_layers=1, outer_layers=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_segments = num_segments\n",
        "        self.inner_gcn = SimpleGCN(in_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=inner_layers)\n",
        "        self.outer_gcn = SimpleGCN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers=outer_layers)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):  # x: [B,T,F]\n",
        "        B, T, F = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "        segments = torch.chunk(x, self.num_segments, dim=1)  # list [B,T_seg,F]\n",
        "        seg_embs = []\n",
        "        for seg in segments:\n",
        "            _, T_seg, _ = seg.shape\n",
        "            A_inner = build_chain_adjacency(T_seg, device)\n",
        "            h_inner = self.inner_gcn(seg, A_inner)       # [B,T_seg,H]\n",
        "            seg_embs.append(h_inner.mean(dim=1))         # [B,H]\n",
        "        seg_graph = torch.stack(seg_embs, dim=1)         # [B,S,H]\n",
        "        A_outer = build_chain_adjacency(self.num_segments, device)\n",
        "        h_outer = self.outer_gcn(seg_graph, A_outer)     # [B,S,H]\n",
        "        return h_outer.mean(dim=1)                       # [B,H]\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 5. Branches with global + GOG representations\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "class TCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, tcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_tcn_layers=3, kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                 dropout=0.3, num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCN(\n",
        "            in_channels=in_features,\n",
        "            hidden_channels=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            num_layers=num_tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=kernel_size,\n",
        "            dropout=dropout\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=tcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):           # x: [B,T,C]\n",
        "        x_tcn = x.transpose(1, 2)   # [B,C,T]\n",
        "        feat = self.tcn(x_tcn)      # [B,H,T]\n",
        "        feat_t = feat.transpose(1, 2)       # [B,T,H]\n",
        "        global_vec = feat_t.mean(dim=1)     # [B,H]\n",
        "        gog_vec = self.gog(feat_t)          # [B,gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return global_vec, gog_vec\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN_GOG_Branch(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, gcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 num_gcn_layers=2, num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_features, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn_layers = nn.ModuleList(\n",
        "            [GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden) for _ in range(num_gcn_layers)]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.gog = GraphOfGraphs(\n",
        "            in_dim=gcn_hidden,\n",
        "            hidden_dim=gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_segments=num_gog_segments,\n",
        "            inner_layers=1,\n",
        "            outer_layers=1\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):           # x: [B,T,C]\n",
        "        B, T, _ = x.shape\n",
        "        device = x.device\n",
        "        A_time = build_chain_adjacency(T, device)\n",
        "        h = self.input_proj(x)      # [B,T,H]\n",
        "        for layer in self.gcn_layers:\n",
        "            h = layer(h, A_time)    # [B,T,H]\n",
        "        global_vec = h.mean(dim=1)  # [B,H]\n",
        "        gog_vec = self.gog(h)       # [B,gog_hidden]\n",
        "        return global_vec, gog_vec\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 6. Final classifier\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, n_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden=96, gcn_hidden=96, gog_hidden=96,\n",
        "                 tcn_layers=3, gcn_layers=2, dropout=0.3,\n",
        "                 num_gog_segments=4):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn_branch = TCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features, tcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_tcn_layers=tcn_layers,\n",
        "            kernel_size=3, dropout=dropout,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.gcn_branch = GCN_GOG_Branch(\n",
        "            in_features, gcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "            num_gcn_layers=gcn_layers,\n",
        "            num_gog_segments=num_gog_segments\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # tcn_global (H) + tcn_gog (G) + gcn_global (H) + gcn_gog (G)\n",
        "        combined_dim = 2*tcn_hidden + 2*gog_hidden\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(combined_dim, 256),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256, 128),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        tcn_global, tcn_gog = self.tcn_branch(x)\n",
        "        gcn_global, gcn_gog = self.gcn_branch(x)\n",
        "        combined = torch.cat([tcn_global, tcn_gog, gcn_global, gcn_gog], dim=-1)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(combined)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 7. Training & evaluation (AMP + early stop)\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion, device, scaler):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds, all_labels = [], []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp_enabled):\n",
        "            logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "            loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if amp_enabled:\n",
        "            scaler.scale(loss).backward()\n",
        "            scaler.step(optimizer)\n",
        "            scaler.update()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            loss.backward()\n",
        "            optimizer.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = logits.argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.detach().cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    return running_loss / len(loader.dataset), accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader, criterion, device):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    all_preds, all_labels = [], []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for X_batch, y_batch in loader:\n",
        "        X_batch = X_batch.to(device)\n",
        "        y_batch = y_batch.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp_enabled):\n",
        "            logits = model(X_batch)\n",
        "            loss = criterion(logits, y_batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item() * X_batch.size(0)\n",
        "        preds = logits.argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "        all_preds.append(preds.cpu().numpy())\n",
        "        all_labels.append(y_batch.cpu().numpy())\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds)\n",
        "    all_labels = np.concatenate(all_labels)\n",
        "    return running_loss / len(loader.dataset), accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "# 8. Run training\n",
        "# ============================================\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GOG_Classifier(\n",
        "    in_features=n_features,\n",
        "    n_classes=n_classes,\n",
        "    tcn_hidden=TCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gcn_hidden=GCN_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    gog_hidden=GOG_HIDDEN,\n",
        "    tcn_layers=3,\n",
        "    gcn_layers=2,\n",
        "    dropout=DROPOUT,\n",
        "    num_gog_segments=NUM_GOG_SEGMENTS\n",
        ").to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(\n",
        "    model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY\n",
        ")\n",
        "scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(\n",
        "    optimizer, mode=\"max\", factor=0.5, patience=5\n",
        ")\n",
        "scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=amp_enabled)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_test_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_state = None\n",
        "patience = 50\n",
        "no_improve = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    train_loss, train_acc = train_one_epoch(\n",
        "        model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion, device, scaler\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    test_loss, test_acc = evaluate(model, test_loader, criterion, device)\n",
        "    old_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"]\n",
        "    scheduler.step(test_acc)\n",
        "    new_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if test_acc > best_test_acc + 1e-4:\n",
        "        best_test_acc = test_acc\n",
        "        best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n",
        "        no_improve = 0\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        no_improve += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    lr_str = f\" LR {old_lr:.2e}->{new_lr:.2e}\" if new_lr != old_lr else \"\"\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | \"\n",
        "        f\"Train Loss={train_loss:.4f} Acc={train_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Test Loss={test_loss:.4f} Acc={test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"Best={best_test_acc*100:5.2f}% | \"\n",
        "        f\"NoImp={no_improve}/{patience}{lr_str}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if no_improve >= patience:\n",
        "        print(\"Early stopping.\")\n",
        "        break\n",
        "\n",
        "if best_state is not None:\n",
        "    model.load_state_dict(best_state)\n",
        "    model.to(device)\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nLoaded best model with Test Accuracy = {best_test_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "**NEW CODE**"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "2fhQehN2Bqq9"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 1) Imports + Config\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np, torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"PhonemeSpectra\"  # <-- CHANGE THIS ONLY\n",
        "\n",
        "EPOCHS = 150\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 16\n",
        "LR = 1e-3\n",
        "WD = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT = 0.4\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp = (device.type==\"cuda\")\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42); np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "print(\"Device =\",device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 2) Load Dataset\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0,0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N,T,C),np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i,:,j] = df.iloc[i,j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "Xtr_df,ytr_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME,\"train\",return_X_y=True)\n",
        "Xte_df,yte_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME,\"test\",return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = df_to_np(Xtr_df)\n",
        "X_test  = df_to_np(Xte_df)\n",
        "N_train,T,C = X_train.shape\n",
        "N_test = X_test.shape[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "# normalize\n",
        "sc = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train = sc.fit_transform(X_train.reshape(-1,C)).reshape(N_train,T,C)\n",
        "X_test  = sc.transform(X_test.reshape(-1,C)).reshape(N_test,T,C)\n",
        "\n",
        "# labels\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(ytr_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(yte_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Shape:\",X_train.shape,\"Classes:\",n_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_train).float(), torch.tensor(y_train).long())),\n",
        "    BATCH_SIZE, True\n",
        ")\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_test).float(), torch.tensor(y_test).long())),\n",
        "    BATCH_SIZE, False\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 3) Graph Utilities\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "_adj = {}\n",
        "def chain_adj(T):\n",
        "    if T in _adj:\n",
        "        return _adj[T].to(device)\n",
        "    A = torch.eye(T)\n",
        "    A[:-1,1:] = A[1:,:-1] = 1\n",
        "    D = torch.diag((A.sum(1))**-0.5)\n",
        "    _adj[T] = (D @ A @ D)\n",
        "    return _adj[T].to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "def segs(T):\n",
        "    return 4 if T<120 else 6 if T<300 else 8\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_SEG = segs(T)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 4) Model Blocks\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class ResTCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, inC, outC):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        in_ch = inC\n",
        "        for i in range(5):\n",
        "            dil = 2**i\n",
        "            layers.append(nn.Conv1d(in_ch, outC, 3, padding=dil, dilation=dil))\n",
        "            layers.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(outC))\n",
        "            layers.append(nn.ReLU())\n",
        "            layers.append(nn.Dropout(DROPOUT))\n",
        "            in_ch = outC\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "        self.res = nn.Conv1d(inC, outC, 1) if inC != outC else nn.Identity()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, C, T]\n",
        "        out = self.net(x)\n",
        "        res = self.res(x)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out + res)\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.l = nn.Linear(F,H)\n",
        "    def forward(self,x,A):\n",
        "        # x: [B, T, F], A: [T, T]\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.l(torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\",A,x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inp = nn.Linear(F,H)\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([GraphConv(H,H) for _ in range(3)])\n",
        "    def forward(self,x,A):\n",
        "        h = F.relu(self.inp(x))\n",
        "        for g in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = g(h,A)\n",
        "        return h\n",
        "\n",
        "class GOG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inner = GCN(F,H)\n",
        "        self.outer = GCN(H,H)\n",
        "    def forward(self,x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, T, F]\n",
        "        segs_list = torch.chunk(x, NUM_SEG, 1)\n",
        "        outs = []\n",
        "        for s in segs_list:\n",
        "            A = chain_adj(s.shape[1])\n",
        "            outs.append(self.inner(s,A).mean(1))\n",
        "        Hs = torch.stack(outs,1)               # [B, NUM_SEG, H]\n",
        "        return self.outer(Hs, chain_adj(NUM_SEG)).mean(1)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 5) Full Hybrid TCN + GCN + GOG Classifier\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn  = ResTCN(inC=C, outC=TCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gcn  = GCN(F=C, H=GCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog1 = GOG(TCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog2 = GOG(GCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "        feat = 2*(TCN_HIDDEN + GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(feat,512), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(512,256), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256,n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    def forward(self,x):\n",
        "        # x: [B, T, F]\n",
        "        B,T,F = x.shape\n",
        "        t = self.tcn(x.transpose(1,2)).transpose(1,2)  # [B, T, TCN_HIDDEN]\n",
        "        g = self.gcn(x, chain_adj(T))                  # [B, T, GCN_HIDDEN]\n",
        "\n",
        "        z = torch.cat([\n",
        "            t.mean(1),          # [B, TCN_HIDDEN]\n",
        "            self.gog1(t),       # [B, GOG_HIDDEN]\n",
        "            g.mean(1),          # [B, GCN_HIDDEN]\n",
        "            self.gog2(g)        # [B, GOG_HIDDEN]\n",
        "        ], 1)\n",
        "        return self.fc(z)\n",
        "\n",
        "model = Model().to(device)\n",
        "print(\"Params:\", sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())/1e6, \"M\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 6) Train + Eval\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "crit = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.1)\n",
        "opt  = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WD)\n",
        "sch  = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(opt, T_max=EPOCHS)\n",
        "\n",
        "best = 0.0\n",
        "for ep in range(1, EPOCHS+1):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    running_loss = 0.0\n",
        "    for xb,yb in train_loader:\n",
        "        xb,yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        opt.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp):\n",
        "            logits = model(xb)\n",
        "            loss = crit(logits, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)\n",
        "        opt.step()\n",
        "        running_loss += loss.item()*xb.size(0)\n",
        "    sch.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # eval\n",
        "    model.eval(); preds=[]; labs=[]\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb,yb in test_loader:\n",
        "            xb = xb.to(device)\n",
        "            pr = model(xb).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "            preds += pr.tolist(); labs += yb.tolist()\n",
        "    acc = accuracy_score(labs,preds)\n",
        "    best = max(best,acc)\n",
        "    avg_loss = running_loss / N_train\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {ep:03d} | loss={avg_loss:7.4f} | acc={acc*100:6.2f}% | best={best*100:6.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n🔥 Best Test Accuracy =\", best*100, \"%\")\n"
      ],
