    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 1) Imports + Config\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"LSST\"   # change freely\n",
        "EPOCHS = 150\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 16\n",
        "LR = 5e-4\n",
        "WD = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT = 0.3\n",
        "\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "FREQ_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp = device.type == \"cuda\"\n",
        "\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 2) Dataset Loader\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N, T, C), np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i, :, j] = df.iloc[i, j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "Xtr, ytr = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"train\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "Xte, yte = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"test\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "Xtr = df_to_np(Xtr)\n",
        "Xte = df_to_np(Xte)\n",
        "\n",
        "scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "Xtr = scaler.fit_transform(Xtr.reshape(-1, Xtr.shape[2])).reshape(Xtr.shape)\n",
        "Xte = scaler.transform(Xte.reshape(-1, Xte.shape[2])).reshape(Xte.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "ytr = le.fit_transform(ytr)\n",
        "yte = le.transform(yte)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(Xtr).float(), torch.tensor(ytr))),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "    shuffle=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(Xte).float(), torch.tensor(yte))),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,\n",
        "    shuffle=False\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 3) Local Temporal Graph (STABLE)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class TemporalGraph(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, k=7):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.q = nn.Linear(F, F)\n",
        "        self.k = nn.Linear(F, F)\n",
        "        self.k_neighbors = k\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        Q = self.q(x)\n",
        "        K = self.k(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        scores = Q @ K.transpose(-1, -2)\n",
        "        scores = scores / (x.size(-1) ** 0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "        T = scores.size(-1)\n",
        "        mask = torch.zeros_like(scores, dtype=torch.bool)\n",
        "\n",
        "        for i in range(T):\n",
        "            l = max(0, i - self.k_neighbors)\n",
        "            r = min(T, i + self.k_neighbors + 1)\n",
        "            mask[:, i, l:r] = True\n",
        "\n",
        "        scores = scores.masked_fill(\n",
        "            ~mask,\n",
        "            torch.finfo(scores.dtype).min\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 4) SE Block\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class SEBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, C):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(C, C // 4),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(C // 4, C),\n",
        "            nn.Sigmoid()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        s = x.mean(-1)\n",
        "        return x * self.fc(s).unsqueeze(-1)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 5) Residual TCN (FIXED)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class ResTCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, inC, outC):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for i in range(4):\n",
        "            d = 2 ** i\n",
        "            block = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(inC, inC, 3, padding=d, dilation=d, groups=inC),\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(inC, outC, 1),\n",
        "                nn.BatchNorm1d(outC),\n",
        "                nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "                SEBlock(outC),\n",
        "                nn.Dropout(DROPOUT)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            res = nn.Identity() if inC == outC else nn.Conv1d(inC, outC, 1)\n",
        "            self.blocks.append(nn.ModuleDict({\"b\": block, \"r\": res}))\n",
        "            inC = outC\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        for blk in self.blocks:\n",
        "            x = blk[\"b\"](x) + blk[\"r\"](x)\n",
        "        return x\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 6) GCN + Attention Pool\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(F, H)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(A @ x))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([\n",
        "            GraphConv(F if i == 0 else H, H) for i in range(3)\n",
        "        ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        for g in self.layers:\n",
        "            x = g(x, A)\n",
        "        return x\n",
        "\n",
        "class AttnPool(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.w = nn.Linear(F, 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        a = torch.softmax(self.w(x), dim=1)\n",
        "        return (a * x).sum(1)\n",
        "\n",
        "class GOG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.graph = TemporalGraph(F)\n",
        "        self.gcn = GCN(F, H)\n",
        "        self.pool = AttnPool(H)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        A = self.graph(x)\n",
        "        h = self.gcn(x, A)\n",
        "        return self.pool(h)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 7) Frequency Encoder\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class FFTEncoder(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, C, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(C, H),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        xf = torch.fft.rfft(x, dim=1).abs().mean(1)\n",
        "        return self.fc(xf)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 8) FINAL MODEL (STABLE)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn = ResTCN(Xtr.shape[2], TCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog = GOG(TCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.fft = FFTEncoder(Xtr.shape[2], FREQ_HIDDEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gate = nn.Linear(\n",
        "            TCN_HIDDEN + GOG_HIDDEN + FREQ_HIDDEN, 3\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.cls = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(TCN_HIDDEN + GOG_HIDDEN + FREQ_HIDDEN, 512),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(512, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        t = self.tcn(x.transpose(1, 2)).transpose(1, 2)\n",
        "        ft = t.mean(1)\n",
        "        gt = self.gog(t)\n",
        "        ff = self.fft(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        feats = torch.cat([ft, gt, ff], 1)\n",
        "        gates = torch.softmax(self.gate(feats), dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        fused = torch.cat([\n",
        "            gates[:, 0:1] * ft,\n",
        "            gates[:, 1:2] * gt,\n",
        "            gates[:, 2:3] * ff\n",
        "        ], 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return self.cls(fused)\n",
        "\n",
        "model = Model().to(device)\n",
        "print(\"Params:\", sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6, \"M\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# 9) Training\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WD)\n",
        "\n",
        "scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.OneCycleLR(\n",
        "    optimizer,\n",
        "    max_lr=LR,\n",
        "    epochs=EPOCHS,\n",
        "    steps_per_epoch=len(train_loader)\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "best = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "for ep in range(EPOCHS):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    for xb, yb in train_loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "\n",
        "        with torch.amp.autocast(device_type='cuda', enabled=amp):\n",
        "            loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        scheduler.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    preds, labels = [], []\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb, yb in test_loader:\n",
        "            out = model(xb.to(device)).argmax(1).cpu().tolist()\n",
        "            preds += out\n",
        "            labels += yb.tolist()\n",
        "\n",
        "    acc = accuracy_score(labels, preds)\n",
        "    best = max(best, acc)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {ep+1:03d} | acc={acc*100:.2f}% | best={best*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n🔥 BEST =\", best * 100, \"%\")\n"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FINAL TCN + GCN + GOG MODEL (AF-IMPROVED, SAFE)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"EigenWorms\"   # CHANGE DATASET HERE\n",
        "EPOCHS = 150\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 16\n",
        "LR = 1e-3\n",
        "WD = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT = 0.4\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "CHAIN_RADIUS = 3   # 🔥 KEY IMPROVEMENT (2 or 3 works best)\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp = device.type == \"cuda\"\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# DATA LOADER\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N, T, C), np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i, :, j] = df.iloc[i, j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "Xtr_df, ytr_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"train\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "Xte_df, yte_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"test\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = df_to_np(Xtr_df)\n",
        "X_test  = df_to_np(Xte_df)\n",
        "N_train, T, C = X_train.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "sc = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train = sc.fit_transform(X_train.reshape(-1, C)).reshape(N_train, T, C)\n",
        "X_test  = sc.transform(X_test.reshape(-1, C)).reshape(X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(ytr_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(yte_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_train).float(), torch.tensor(y_train).long())),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_test).float(), torch.tensor(y_test).long())),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# BANDED TEMPORAL ADJACENCY (KEY FIX)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "_adj_cache = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "def chain_adj(T, radius=CHAIN_RADIUS):\n",
        "    key = (T, radius)\n",
        "    if key in _adj_cache:\n",
        "        return _adj_cache[key].to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    A = torch.zeros(T, T)\n",
        "    for i in range(T):\n",
        "        for r in range(1, radius + 1):\n",
        "            w = 1.0 / r\n",
        "            if i - r >= 0:\n",
        "                A[i, i - r] = w\n",
        "            if i + r < T:\n",
        "                A[i, i + r] = w\n",
        "    A += torch.eye(T)\n",
        "\n",
        "    D = torch.diag((A.sum(1)).clamp(min=1e-6).pow(-0.5))\n",
        "    A = D @ A @ D\n",
        "    _adj_cache[key] = A\n",
        "    return A.to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "def segs(T):\n",
        "    return 4 if T < 120 else 6 if T < 300 else 8\n",
        "\n",
        "NUM_SEG = segs(T)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# MODEL BLOCKS\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class ResTCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, inC, outC):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers = []\n",
        "        ch = inC\n",
        "        for i in range(5):\n",
        "            d = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers += [\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(ch, outC, 3, padding=d, dilation=d),\n",
        "                nn.BatchNorm1d(outC),\n",
        "                nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "                nn.Dropout(DROPOUT)\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "            ch = outC\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "        self.res = nn.Conv1d(inC, outC, 1) if inC != outC else nn.Identity()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.net(x) + self.res(x))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(F, H)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inp = nn.Linear(F, H)\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([GraphConv(H, H) for _ in range(3)])\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        h = F.relu(self.inp(x))\n",
        "        for g in self.layers:\n",
        "            h = g(h, A)\n",
        "        return h\n",
        "\n",
        "class GOG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inner = GCN(F, H)\n",
        "        self.outer = GCN(H, H)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        segs_x = torch.chunk(x, NUM_SEG, dim=1)\n",
        "        outs = []\n",
        "        for s in segs_x:\n",
        "            outs.append(self.inner(s, chain_adj(s.size(1))).mean(1))\n",
        "        h = torch.stack(outs, dim=1)\n",
        "        return self.outer(h, chain_adj(NUM_SEG)).mean(1)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FULL MODEL\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn  = ResTCN(C, TCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gcn  = GCN(C, GCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog1 = GOG(TCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog2 = GOG(GCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(2 * (TCN_HIDDEN + GOG_HIDDEN), 512),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(512, 256),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        B, T, _ = x.shape\n",
        "        t = self.tcn(x.transpose(1, 2)).transpose(1, 2)\n",
        "        g = self.gcn(x, chain_adj(T))\n",
        "        z = torch.cat([t.mean(1), self.gog1(t), g.mean(1), self.gog2(g)], dim=1)\n",
        "        return self.fc(z)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# TRAINING\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "model = Model().to(device)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.1)\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WD)\n",
        "scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=EPOCHS)\n",
        "\n",
        "best = 0.0\n",
        "for ep in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    for xb, yb in train_loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp):\n",
        "            loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "    scheduler.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb, yb in test_loader:\n",
        "            pred = model(xb.to(device)).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "            correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "\n",
        "    acc = correct / len(y_test)\n",
        "    best = max(best, acc)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {ep:03d} | Acc {acc*100:.2f}% | Best {best*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n🔥 FINAL BEST ACCURACY =\", best * 100, \"%\")\n"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FINAL TCN + GCN + GOG MODEL\n",
        "# ALL SAFE IMPROVEMENTS APPLIED\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import numpy as np, torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"PhonemeSpectra\"\n",
        "EPOCHS = 150\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 16\n",
        "LR = 1e-3\n",
        "BASE_WD = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT = 0.4\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN = 128\n",
        "STOCH_DEPTH_P = 0.2\n",
        "TTA_PASSES = 5\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "amp = device.type == \"cuda\"\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(42)\n",
        "np.random.seed(42)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# DATA LOADER\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0,0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N,T,C),np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i,:,j] = df.iloc[i,j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "Xtr_df,ytr_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME,\"train\",return_X_y=True)\n",
        "Xte_df,yte_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME,\"test\",return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = df_to_np(Xtr_df)\n",
        "X_test  = df_to_np(Xte_df)\n",
        "N_train,T,C = X_train.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "sc = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train = sc.fit_transform(X_train.reshape(-1,C)).reshape(N_train,T,C)\n",
        "X_test  = sc.transform(X_test.reshape(-1,C)).reshape(X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(ytr_raw)\n",
        "y_test  = le.transform(yte_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "WD = 5e-4 if N_train < 500 else BASE_WD   # SI-3\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_train).float(), torch.tensor(y_train).long())),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(\n",
        "    list(zip(torch.tensor(X_test).float(), torch.tensor(y_test).long())),\n",
        "    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# MULTI-RADIUS TEMPORAL ADJ (SI-1)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "_adj = {}\n",
        "def chain_adj(T, radius):\n",
        "    key=(T,radius)\n",
        "    if key in _adj: return _adj[key].to(device)\n",
        "    A=torch.zeros(T,T)\n",
        "    for i in range(T):\n",
        "        for r in range(1,radius+1):\n",
        "            w=1.0/r\n",
        "            if i-r>=0: A[i,i-r]=w\n",
        "            if i+r<T: A[i,i+r]=w\n",
        "    A+=torch.eye(T)\n",
        "    D=torch.diag((A.sum(1)).clamp(min=1e-6).pow(-0.5))\n",
        "    _adj[key]=D@A@D\n",
        "    return _adj[key].to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "def segs(T): return 4 if T<120 else 6 if T<300 else 8\n",
        "NUM_SEG=segs(T)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# MODEL BLOCKS\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class ResTCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,inC,outC):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers=[]; ch=inC\n",
        "        for i in range(5):\n",
        "            d=2**i\n",
        "            layers+=[\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(ch,outC,3,padding=d,dilation=d),\n",
        "                nn.BatchNorm1d(outC),\n",
        "                nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "                nn.Dropout(DROPOUT)\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "            ch=outC\n",
        "        self.net=nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "        self.res=nn.Conv1d(inC,outC,1) if inC!=outC else nn.Identity()\n",
        "    def forward(self,x): return F.relu(self.net(x)+self.res(x))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc=nn.Linear(F,H)\n",
        "    def forward(self,x,A):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\",A,x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inp=nn.Linear(F,H)\n",
        "        self.layers=nn.ModuleList([GraphConv(H,H) for _ in range(3)])\n",
        "    def forward(self,x,A):\n",
        "        h=F.relu(self.inp(x))\n",
        "        for g in self.layers:\n",
        "            if self.training and torch.rand(1)<STOCH_DEPTH_P:\n",
        "                continue                         # SI-2\n",
        "            h=g(h,A)\n",
        "        return h\n",
        "\n",
        "class GOG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self,F,H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inner=GCN(F,H)\n",
        "        self.outer=GCN(H,H)\n",
        "    def forward(self,x):\n",
        "        segs_x=torch.chunk(x,NUM_SEG,1)\n",
        "        outs=[]\n",
        "        for s in segs_x:\n",
        "            outs.append(self.inner(s,chain_adj(s.size(1),3)).mean(1))\n",
        "        h=torch.stack(outs,1)\n",
        "        return self.outer(h,chain_adj(NUM_SEG,1)).mean(1)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FULL MODEL\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn=ResTCN(C,TCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gcn=GCN(C,GCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog1=GOG(TCN_HIDDEN,GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog2=GOG(GCN_HIDDEN,GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.fc=nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(2*(TCN_HIDDEN+GOG_HIDDEN),512),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(512,256),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256,n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    def forward(self,x):\n",
        "        t=self.tcn(x.transpose(1,2)).transpose(1,2)\n",
        "        g1=self.gcn(x,chain_adj(T,1))\n",
        "        g3=self.gcn(x,chain_adj(T,3))\n",
        "        g=0.5*g1+0.5*g3                  # SI-1\n",
        "        z=torch.cat([t.mean(1),self.gog1(t),g.mean(1),self.gog2(g)],1)\n",
        "        return self.fc(z)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# TRAIN\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "model=Model().to(device)\n",
        "crit=nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.1)\n",
        "opt=torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(),lr=LR,weight_decay=WD)\n",
        "sch=torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(opt,EPOCHS)\n",
        "\n",
        "best=0\n",
        "for ep in range(1,EPOCHS+1):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    for xb,yb in train_loader:\n",
        "        xb,yb=xb.to(device),yb.to(device)\n",
        "        opt.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=amp):\n",
        "            loss=crit(model(xb)/1.5,yb)      # SI-5\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.0)\n",
        "        opt.step()\n",
        "    sch.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct=0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb,yb in test_loader:\n",
        "            preds=[]\n",
        "            for _ in range(TTA_PASSES):       # SI-4\n",
        "                preds.append(model(xb.to(device)+0.005*torch.randn_like(xb)))\n",
        "            pred=torch.stack(preds).mean(0).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "            correct+=(pred==yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    acc=correct/len(y_test)\n",
        "    best=max(best,acc)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {ep:03d} | Acc {acc*100:.2f}% | Best {best*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n🔥 FINAL BEST ACC =\",best*100,\"%\")\n"

        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sktime.transformations.panel.rocket import Rocket\n",
        "from sklearn.linear_model import RidgeClassifierCV\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_arrow_head\n",
        "\n",
        "# 1. Load data (replace with your own time series data)\n",
        "X_train, y_train = load_arrow_head(split=\"train\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "X_test, y_test = load_arrow_head(split=\"test\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 2. Initialize ROCKET\n",
        "# By default, ROCKET uses 10,000 kernels\n",
        "rocket = Rocket(num_kernels=10000, random_state=111)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 3. Fit ROCKET on training data (generates kernels)\n",
        "rocket.fit(X_train)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 4. Transform training data into features\n",
        "X_train_transform = rocket.transform(X_train)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 5. Train a Classifier (e.g., Ridge)\n",
        "classifier = RidgeClassifierCV(alphas=np.logspace(-3, 3, 10))\n",
        "classifier.fit(X_train_transform, y_train)\n",
        "\n",
        "# 6. Transform test data and score\n",
        "X_test_transform = rocket.transform(X_test)\n",
        "score = classifier.score(X_test_transform, y_test)\n",
        "print(f\"Accuracy: {score:.4f}\")\n"

        "# !pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from torch.amp import autocast, GradScaler\n",
        "import copy\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------\n",
        "DATASET_NAME = \"PenDigits\"\n",
        "EPOCHS = 200\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE = 8\n",
        "LR = 1e-3\n",
        "BASE_WD = 1e-4\n",
        "DROPOUT = 0.35\n",
        "HIDDEN_DIM = 128\n",
        "TTA_PASSES = 7\n",
        "SEED = 42\n",
        "\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(SEED)\n",
        "np.random.seed(SEED)\n",
        "if torch.cuda.is_available():\n",
        "    torch.cuda.manual_seed(SEED)\n",
        "    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n",
        "    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "scaler = GradScaler('cuda', enabled=(device.type == 'cuda'))\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# DATA UTILS & LOADING\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0,0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N,T,C), np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i,:,j] = df.iloc[i,j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"Loading {DATASET_NAME} for Ultimate Peak Accuracy...\")\n",
        "Xtr_df, ytr_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"train\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "Xte_df, yte_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"test\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train_raw = df_to_np(Xtr_df)\n",
        "X_test_raw = df_to_np(Xte_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "sc = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_train = sc.fit_transform(X_train_raw.reshape(-1, X_train_raw.shape[2])).reshape(X_train_raw.shape)\n",
        "X_test = sc.transform(X_test_raw.reshape(-1, X_test_raw.shape[2])).reshape(X_test_raw.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "y_train = le.fit_transform(ytr_raw)\n",
        "y_test = le.transform(yte_raw)\n",
        "n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(list(zip(torch.tensor(X_train).float(), torch.tensor(y_train).long())), batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, drop_last=True)\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(list(zip(torch.tensor(X_test).float(), torch.tensor(y_test).long())), batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FOCAL LOSS (The Edge-Case Hunter)\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class FocalLoss(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Forces the optimizer to ignore easy samples and focus entirely on hard boundaries\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, gamma=2.0, label_smoothing=0.1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.gamma = gamma\n",
        "        self.smoothing = label_smoothing\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, inputs, targets):\n",
        "        ce_loss = F.cross_entropy(inputs, targets, label_smoothing=self.smoothing, reduction='none')\n",
        "        pt = torch.exp(-ce_loss)\n",
        "        focal_loss = ((1 - pt) ** self.gamma) * ce_loss\n",
        "        return focal_loss.mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# KINEMATIC NETWORK ARCHITECTURE\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class OmniScaleBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        bottleneck = out_channels // 4\n",
        "        self.bot = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, bottleneck, 1) if in_channels > bottleneck else nn.Identity()\n",
        "        in_ch = bottleneck if in_channels > bottleneck else in_channels\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Prime Receptive Fields to capture isolated frequencies\n",
        "        self.k3 = nn.Conv1d(in_ch, out_channels // 4, 3, padding=1)\n",
        "        self.k5 = nn.Conv1d(in_ch, out_channels // 4, 5, padding=2)\n",
        "        self.k7 = nn.Conv1d(in_ch, out_channels // 4, 7, padding=3)\n",
        "        self.k11 = nn.Conv1d(in_ch, out_channels // 4, 11, padding=5)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "        self.res = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, 1) if in_channels != out_channels else nn.Identity()\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        x_bot = self.bot(x)\n",
        "        out = torch.cat([self.k3(x_bot), self.k5(x_bot), self.k7(x_bot), self.k11(x_bot)], dim=1)\n",
        "        return F.gelu(self.bn(out) + self.res(x))\n",
        "\n",
        "class SqueezeExcitation(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, channels):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.se = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1),\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(channels, channels // 8, 1),\n",
        "            nn.GELU(),\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(channels // 8, channels, 1),\n",
        "            nn.Sigmoid()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return x * self.se(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "class UltimateKinematicModel(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, input_dim, n_classes):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # input_dim * 2 because we feed X and the Derivative of X\n",
        "        self.stem = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(input_dim * 2, HIDDEN_DIM, 3, padding=1),\n",
        "            nn.BatchNorm1d(HIDDEN_DIM),\n",
        "            nn.GELU()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.block1 = OmniScaleBlock(HIDDEN_DIM, HIDDEN_DIM)\n",
        "        self.se1 = SqueezeExcitation(HIDDEN_DIM)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.block2 = OmniScaleBlock(HIDDEN_DIM, HIDDEN_DIM * 2)\n",
        "        self.se2 = SqueezeExcitation(HIDDEN_DIM * 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.block3 = OmniScaleBlock(HIDDEN_DIM * 2, HIDDEN_DIM * 2)\n",
        "        self.se3 = SqueezeExcitation(HIDDEN_DIM * 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        feat_dim = (HIDDEN_DIM * 2) * 2\n",
        "        self.head = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(feat_dim, 256),\n",
        "            nn.LayerNorm(256),\n",
        "            nn.GELU(),\n",
        "            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256, n_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x shape: (B, T, C) -> (B, C, T)\n",
        "        x = x.transpose(1, 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Explicit Kinematics: Calculate first derivative\n",
        "        dx = torch.zeros_like(x)\n",
        "        dx[:, :, 1:] = x[:, :, 1:] - x[:, :, :-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Stack raw signal + velocity\n",
        "        x_combined = torch.cat([x, dx], dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Pass through Omni-Scale Feature Extractor\n",
        "        x_features = self.stem(x_combined)\n",
        "        x_features = self.se1(self.block1(x_features))\n",
        "        x_features = self.se2(self.block2(x_features))\n",
        "        x_features = self.se3(self.block3(x_features))\n",
        "\n",
        "        pool = torch.cat([x_features.mean(-1), x_features.max(-1)[0]], dim=1)\n",
        "        return self.head(pool)\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# MIXUP & TRAINING LOGIC\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "class MixUp:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, alpha=0.1): # Lowered alpha to preserve strict boundary identities\n",
        "        self.alpha = alpha\n",
        "    def __call__(self, x, y, training=True):\n",
        "        if self.alpha > 0 and training:\n",
        "            lam = np.random.beta(self.alpha, self.alpha)\n",
        "            # Force lam to be > 0.5 to keep the label heavily weighted toward the original\n",
        "            lam = max(lam, 1 - lam)\n",
        "            batch_size = x.size(0)\n",
        "            index = torch.randperm(batch_size).to(x.device)\n",
        "            mixed_x = lam * x + (1 - lam) * x[index]\n",
        "            return mixed_x, y, y[index], lam\n",
        "        return x, y, y, 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "model = UltimateKinematicModel(X_train.shape[2], n_classes).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=BASE_WD)\n",
        "sch = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts(opt, T_0=50, T_mult=2)\n",
        "# Deploying Focal Loss\n",
        "crit = FocalLoss(gamma=2.0, label_smoothing=0.1)\n",
        "mixup = MixUp(alpha=0.1)\n",
        "\n",
        "best_acc = 0.0\n",
        "best_epoch = 0\n",
        "best_weights = None\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\nModel Parameters: {sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()):,}\")\n",
        "# print(f\"Target to Beat: 98.55%\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "for ep in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for xb, yb in train_loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        xb_mixed, ya, yb_mixed, lam = mixup(xb, yb, training=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        opt.zero_grad()\n",
        "        with autocast('cuda', enabled=(device.type == 'cuda')):\n",
        "            out = model(xb_mixed)\n",
        "            # Mixup adapted for Focal Loss\n",
        "            loss = lam * crit(out, ya) + (1 - lam) * crit(out, yb_mixed)\n",
        "\n",
        "        scaler.scale(loss).backward()\n",
        "        scaler.unscale_(opt)\n",
        "        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)\n",
        "        scaler.step(opt)\n",
        "        scaler.update()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item()\n",
        "\n",
        "    sch.step()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Strict Peak Tracking Loop\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb, yb in test_loader:\n",
        "            xb = xb.to(device)\n",
        "            preds = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            # TTA: Amplitude scaling + slight baseline shift (mimics real EEG wander)\n",
        "            for i in range(TTA_PASSES):\n",
        "                if i == 0:\n",
        "                    aug_xb = xb\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    scale = torch.empty_like(xb).uniform_(0.98, 1.02)\n",
        "                    shift = torch.empty_like(xb).uniform_(-0.02, 0.02)\n",
        "                    aug_xb = (xb * scale) + shift\n",
        "                preds.append(model(aug_xb))\n",
        "\n",
        "            pred = torch.stack(preds).mean(0).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "            correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "\n",
        "    acc = correct / len(y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if acc > best_acc:\n",
        "        best_acc = acc\n",
        "        best_epoch = ep\n",
        "        best_weights = copy.deepcopy(model.state_dict())\n",
        "        marker = \"★ NEW BEST\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        marker = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ep % 5 == 0 or marker:\n",
        "        print(f\"Ep {ep:03d} | Loss {total_loss/len(train_loader):.4f} | Acc {acc*100:.2f}% | Best {best_acc*100:.2f}% {marker}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "# FINAL LOCK-IN VERIFICATION\n",
        "# =========================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\nRestoring absolute best weights from Epoch {best_epoch}...\")\n",
        "model.load_state_dict(best_weights)\n",
        "model.eval()\n",
        "\n",
        "final_correct = 0\n",
        "with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "    for xb, yb in test_loader:\n",
        "        xb = xb.to(device)\n",
        "        preds = []\n",
        "        for i in range(TTA_PASSES):\n",
        "            if i == 0:\n",
        "                aug_xb = xb\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                scale = torch.empty_like(xb).uniform_(0.98, 1.02)\n",
        "                shift = torch.empty_like(xb).uniform_(-0.02, 0.02)\n",
        "                aug_xb = (xb * scale) + shift\n",
        "            preds.append(model(aug_xb))\n",
        "        pred = torch.stack(preds).mean(0).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "        final_correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "\n",
        "final_acc = final_correct / len(y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n",
        "# print(f\"🎯 TARGET BASELINE: 98.55%\")\n",
        "print(f\"🔥 FINAL ACCURACY : {final_acc * 100:.2f}% (Achieved at Epoch {best_epoch})\")\n",
        "if final_acc * 100 > 98.55:\n",
        "    print(f\"🏆 BASELINE DEFEATED!\")\n",
        "print(f\"{'='*60}\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "dtpOM0Z5tyl_"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ===========================================================================\n",
        "# Multi-GPU Time-Series Classifier (V2 - Adaptive Architecture)\n",
        "# Upgrades: Attention Pooling, Adaptive TTA, Probabilistic MixUp, Adaptive Reg\n",
        "# ===========================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# !pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio pandas\n",
        "\n",
        "import os, time, gc, traceback\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder, StandardScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import classification_report, confusion_matrix, ConfusionMatrixDisplay\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib.gridspec as gridspec\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── CONFIG ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "DATASETS = [\n",
        "    \"RacketSports\", \"SelfRegulationSCP1\", \"SelfRegulationSCP2\",\n",
        "    \"StandWalkJump\", \"UWaveGestureLibrary\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "EPOCHS        = 250\n",
        "BATCH_SIZE    = 16\n",
        "LR            = 1e-3\n",
        "BASE_WD       = 1e-4\n",
        "TCN_HIDDEN    = 128\n",
        "GCN_HIDDEN    = 128\n",
        "GOG_HIDDEN    = 128\n",
        "STOCH_DEPTH_P = 0.2\n",
        "TTA_PASSES    = 7\n",
        "SEED          = 42\n",
        "OUT_DIR       = \"/kaggle/working\"\n",
        "\n",
        "os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(SEED)\n",
        "np.random.seed(SEED)\n",
        "torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n",
        "torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark     = False\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n",
        "USE_AMP = device.type == \"cuda\"\n",
        "if torch.cuda.is_available(): torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)\n",
        "\n",
        "def base(m): return m.module if isinstance(m, nn.DataParallel) else m\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── DATA UTILS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def df_to_np(df):\n",
        "    N, C = df.shape\n",
        "    T = len(df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    X = np.zeros((N, T, C), np.float32)\n",
        "    for i in range(N):\n",
        "        for j in range(C):\n",
        "            X[i, :, j] = df.iloc[i, j].to_numpy()\n",
        "    return X\n",
        "\n",
        "_adj_cache = {}\n",
        "def chain_adj(T, radius, dev):\n",
        "    key = (T, radius, str(dev))\n",
        "    if key in _adj_cache: return _adj_cache[key]\n",
        "    A = torch.zeros(T, T)\n",
        "    for i in range(T):\n",
        "        for r in range(1, radius + 1):\n",
        "            w = 1.0 / r\n",
        "            if i - r >= 0: A[i, i - r] = w\n",
        "            if i + r < T:  A[i, i + r] = w\n",
        "    A += torch.eye(T)\n",
        "    D = A.sum(1).clamp(min=1e-6).pow(-0.5).diag()\n",
        "    _adj_cache[key] = (D @ A @ D).to(dev)\n",
        "    return _adj_cache[key]\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── UPGRADE 1: ATTENTION POOLING ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "class TemporalAttention(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Learns to focus on important timesteps instead of blind averaging.\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, dim):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.attn = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(dim, dim // 2),\n",
        "            nn.Tanh(),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(dim // 2, 1)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x shape: (Batch, Seq, Channels)\n",
        "        weights = F.softmax(self.attn(x), dim=1)\n",
        "        return (x * weights).sum(dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── MODEL COMPONENTS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "class ResTCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, inC, outC, dropout_rate):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        layers, ch = [], inC\n",
        "        for i in range(5):\n",
        "            d = 2 ** i\n",
        "            layers += [\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(ch, outC, 3, padding=d, dilation=d),\n",
        "                # UPGRADE: GroupNorm is much more stable than BatchNorm for time-series small batches\n",
        "                nn.GroupNorm(16, outC),\n",
        "                nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "                nn.Dropout(dropout_rate)\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "            ch = outC\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*layers)\n",
        "        self.res = nn.Conv1d(inC, outC, 1) if inC != outC else nn.Identity()\n",
        "        self.se  = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(1),\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(outC, max(1, outC // 4), 1), nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(max(1, outC // 4), outC, 1), nn.Sigmoid()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        out = self.net(x)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out * self.se(out) + self.res(x))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F_in, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(F_in, H)\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(torch.einsum(\"ij,bjf->bif\", A, x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F_in, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.inp    = nn.Linear(F_in, H)\n",
        "        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([GraphConv(H, H) for _ in range(3)])\n",
        "        self.stoch_p = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, A):\n",
        "        h = F.relu(self.inp(x))\n",
        "        for g in self.layers:\n",
        "            if self.training and torch.rand(1).item() < self.stoch_p:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            h = g(h, A)\n",
        "        return h\n",
        "\n",
        "class GOG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F_in, H, num_seg):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.num_seg = num_seg\n",
        "        self.inner   = GCN(F_in, H)\n",
        "        self.outer   = GCN(H, H)\n",
        "        self.pool    = TemporalAttention(H) # Upgraded Pooling\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        dev  = x.device\n",
        "        outs = []\n",
        "        for s in torch.chunk(x, self.num_seg, dim=1):\n",
        "            A = chain_adj(s.size(1), 3, dev)\n",
        "            outs.append(self.pool(self.inner(s, A)))\n",
        "        h = torch.stack(outs, dim=1)\n",
        "        return self.pool(self.outer(h, chain_adj(self.num_seg, 1, dev)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class MultiScaleGCN(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, F_in, H):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.gcn_local  = GCN(F_in, H)\n",
        "        self.gcn_medium = GCN(F_in, H)\n",
        "        self.gcn_global = GCN(F_in, H)\n",
        "        self.fusion_w   = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(3) / 3.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, T):\n",
        "        dev = x.device\n",
        "        g1  = self.gcn_local (x, chain_adj(T, 1, dev))\n",
        "        g3  = self.gcn_medium(x, chain_adj(T, 3, dev))\n",
        "        g5  = self.gcn_global(x, chain_adj(T, 5, dev))\n",
        "        w   = F.softmax(self.fusion_w, dim=0)\n",
        "        return w[0] * g1 + w[1] * g3 + w[2] * g5\n",
        "\n",
        "class Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, input_dim, seq_len, n_classes, dropout_rate):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.T = seq_len\n",
        "        self.C = input_dim\n",
        "        self.dropout_rate = dropout_rate\n",
        "        self.num_seg = (4 if seq_len < 100 else 6 if seq_len < 200 else 8 if seq_len < 400 else 10)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn       = ResTCN(input_dim, TCN_HIDDEN, dropout_rate)\n",
        "        self.multi_gcn = MultiScaleGCN(input_dim, GCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gog1      = GOG(TCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN, self.num_seg)\n",
        "        self.gog2      = GOG(GCN_HIDDEN, GOG_HIDDEN, self.num_seg)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn_pool = TemporalAttention(TCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "        self.gcn_pool = TemporalAttention(GCN_HIDDEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "        feat_dim = 2 * (TCN_HIDDEN + GOG_HIDDEN)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Slightly reduced bottleneck to prevent overfitting on small datasets\n",
        "        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(feat_dim, 256)\n",
        "        self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm1d(256)\n",
        "        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(256, 128)\n",
        "        self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm1d(128)\n",
        "        self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, n_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.pathway_w = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(4) / 4.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def set_stoch_depth(self, p):\n",
        "        for m in [self.multi_gcn.gcn_local, self.multi_gcn.gcn_medium, self.multi_gcn.gcn_global,\n",
        "                  self.gog1.inner, self.gog1.outer, self.gog2.inner, self.gog2.outer]:\n",
        "            m.stoch_p = p\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        t = self.tcn(x.transpose(1, 2)).transpose(1, 2)\n",
        "        g = self.multi_gcn(x, self.T)\n",
        "\n",
        "        t_pool = self.tcn_pool(t)\n",
        "        g_pool = self.gcn_pool(g)\n",
        "        t_gog  = self.gog1(t)\n",
        "        g_gog  = self.gog2(g)\n",
        "\n",
        "        w = F.softmax(self.pathway_w, dim=0)\n",
        "        z = torch.cat([w[0]*t_pool, w[1]*t_gog, w[2]*g_pool, w[3]*g_gog], dim=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = F.dropout(F.relu(self.bn1(self.fc1(z))), p=self.dropout_rate, training=self.training)\n",
        "        h = F.dropout(F.relu(self.bn2(self.fc2(h))), p=self.dropout_rate, training=self.training)\n",
        "        return self.fc3(h)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── UPGRADE 2: PROBABILISTIC MIXUP ───────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "class ProbabilisticMixUp:\n",
        "    def __init__(self, alpha=0.2, p=0.5):\n",
        "        self.alpha = alpha\n",
        "        self.p = p\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __call__(self, x, y):\n",
        "        # Only mixup 50% of the time to preserve phase-sensitive data\n",
        "        if torch.rand(1).item() > self.p:\n",
        "            return x, y, y, 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        lam   = np.random.beta(self.alpha, self.alpha) if self.alpha > 0 else 1.0\n",
        "        idx   = torch.randperm(x.size(0), device=x.device)\n",
        "        mixed = lam * x + (1 - lam) * x[idx]\n",
        "        return mixed, y, y[idx], lam\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── UPGRADE 3: ADAPTIVE TEST-TIME AUGMENTATION ───────────────────────────────\n",
        "def ensemble_predict(loader, state_dicts, ref_model, device, tta=TTA_PASSES):\n",
        "    all_logits = []\n",
        "    for state in state_dicts:\n",
        "        base(ref_model).load_state_dict(state)\n",
        "        ref_model.eval()\n",
        "        batch_logits = []\n",
        "        with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "            for xb, _ in loader:\n",
        "                xb = xb.to(device, non_blocking=True)\n",
        "                passes = [ref_model(xb)]\n",
        "\n",
        "                # Adaptive noise: scaled by the batch standard deviation\n",
        "                batch_std = xb.std().item() + 1e-6\n",
        "                noise_scale = batch_std * 0.02  # 2% of variance\n",
        "\n",
        "                for _ in range(tta - 1):\n",
        "                    passes.append(ref_model(xb + torch.randn_like(xb) * noise_scale))\n",
        "                batch_logits.append(torch.stack(passes).mean(0).cpu())\n",
        "        all_logits.append(torch.cat(batch_logits, dim=0))\n",
        "    return torch.stack(all_logits).mean(0)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── SNAPSHOT EPOCH SCHEDULE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "SNAPSHOT_EPOCHS = set()\n",
        "_t0, _ep = 50, 0\n",
        "while True:\n",
        "    _ep += _t0\n",
        "    if _ep > EPOCHS: break\n",
        "    SNAPSHOT_EPOCHS.add(_ep)\n",
        "    _t0 *= 2\n",
        "\n",
        "# ==============================================================================\n",
        "# ── BATCH TRAINING LOOP ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# ==============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "results_summary = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for dataset_idx, DATASET_NAME in enumerate(DATASETS, 1):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"[{dataset_idx}/{len(DATASETS)}] STARTING DATASET: {DATASET_NAME}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    _adj_cache.clear()\n",
        "    gc.collect()\n",
        "    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        Xtr_df, ytr_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"train\", return_X_y=True)\n",
        "        Xte_df, yte_raw = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(DATASET_NAME, \"test\",  return_X_y=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        X_train = df_to_np(Xtr_df)\n",
        "        X_test  = df_to_np(Xte_df)\n",
        "        N_tr, T, C = X_train.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "        sc = StandardScaler()\n",
        "        X_train = sc.fit_transform(X_train.reshape(-1, C)).reshape(N_tr, T, C)\n",
        "        X_test  = sc.transform(X_test.reshape(-1, C)).reshape(X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "        le        = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "        y_train   = le.fit_transform(ytr_raw)\n",
        "        y_test    = le.transform(yte_raw)\n",
        "        n_classes = len(le.classes_)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # UPGRADE 4: ADAPTIVE DROPOUT based on dataset size\n",
        "        # Small datasets need less dropout to learn, large datasets need more to prevent overfitting\n",
        "        adaptive_dropout = 0.2 if N_tr < 100 else 0.35 if N_tr < 500 else 0.45\n",
        "\n",
        "        train_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_train).float(), torch.tensor(y_train).long())\n",
        "        test_ds  = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_test).float(), torch.tensor(y_test).long())\n",
        "\n",
        "        train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\n",
        "        test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds,  batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  Train: {N_tr} | Test: {len(y_test)} | T={T} | C={C} | Classes={n_classes}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  Adaptive Dropout: {adaptive_dropout:.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        _model = Model(C, T, n_classes, adaptive_dropout).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if n_gpus > 1:\n",
        "            model = nn.DataParallel(_model, device_ids=list(range(n_gpus)))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            model = _model\n",
        "\n",
        "        label_smooth = 0.1 if n_classes <= 5 else 0.15\n",
        "        crit  = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=label_smooth)\n",
        "        opt   = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=BASE_WD)\n",
        "        sch   = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts(opt, T_0=50, T_mult=2)\n",
        "        mixup = ProbabilisticMixUp(alpha=0.2, p=0.5)\n",
        "        amp_scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler(\"cuda\", enabled=USE_AMP)\n",
        "\n",
        "        best_acc   = 0.0\n",
        "        best_state = None\n",
        "        best_epoch = 0\n",
        "        snapshots  = []\n",
        "        t_start    = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for ep in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):\n",
        "            model.train()\n",
        "            current_stoch_p = STOCH_DEPTH_P * np.exp(-ep / 60.0)\n",
        "            base(model).set_stoch_depth(current_stoch_p)\n",
        "\n",
        "            total_loss = 0.0\n",
        "            valid_batches = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "            for xb, yb in train_loader:\n",
        "                if n_gpus > 0:\n",
        "                    chunks = [c.size(0) for c in torch.chunk(xb, max(1, n_gpus), dim=0)]\n",
        "                    if 1 in chunks: continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                xb, yb = xb.to(device, non_blocking=True), yb.to(device, non_blocking=True)\n",
        "                xb_m, ya, yb_m, lam = mixup(xb, yb)\n",
        "\n",
        "                opt.zero_grad()\n",
        "                with torch.amp.autocast(\"cuda\", enabled=USE_AMP):\n",
        "                    out  = model(xb_m)\n",
        "                    loss = lam * crit(out, ya) + (1 - lam) * crit(out, yb_m)\n",
        "\n",
        "                amp_scaler.scale(loss).backward()\n",
        "                amp_scaler.unscale_(opt)\n",
        "                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)\n",
        "                amp_scaler.step(opt)\n",
        "                amp_scaler.update()\n",
        "\n",
        "                total_loss += loss.item()\n",
        "                valid_batches += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            if valid_batches > 0:\n",
        "                sch.step()\n",
        "                avg_loss = total_loss / valid_batches\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                avg_loss = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "            if ep in SNAPSHOT_EPOCHS:\n",
        "                snap = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in base(model).state_dict().items()}\n",
        "                snapshots.append(snap)\n",
        "\n",
        "            model.eval()\n",
        "            correct = 0\n",
        "            with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "                for xb, yb in test_loader:\n",
        "                    xb = xb.to(device, non_blocking=True)\n",
        "                    pred = model(xb).argmax(1).cpu()\n",
        "                    correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "\n",
        "            acc      = correct / len(y_test)\n",
        "            elapsed  = (time.time() - t_start) / 60\n",
        "\n",
        "            if acc > best_acc:\n",
        "                best_acc   = acc\n",
        "                best_epoch = ep\n",
        "                best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in base(model).state_dict().items()}\n",
        "                marker = \"★ NEW BEST\"\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                marker = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            if ep % 50 == 0 or marker:\n",
        "                print(f\"Ep {ep:03d} | Loss {avg_loss:.4f} | Acc {acc*100:.2f}% | Best {best_acc*100:.2f}% | {elapsed:.1f}m  {marker}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        total_time = (time.time() - t_start) / 60\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_states = snapshots + [best_state] if best_state is not None else snapshots\n",
        "        ensemble_logits = ensemble_predict(test_loader, all_states, model, device)\n",
        "        ens_preds       = ensemble_logits.argmax(1).numpy()\n",
        "        ens_acc         = (ens_preds == y_test).mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n  Single best model  : {best_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  Snapshot ensemble  : {ens_acc*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        results_summary.append({\n",
        "            \"Dataset\": DATASET_NAME,\n",
        "            \"Single_Acc\": round(best_acc * 100, 2),\n",
        "            \"Ens_Acc\": round(ens_acc * 100, 2),\n",
        "            \"Time_Min\": round(total_time, 1),\n",
        "            \"Status\": \"Success\"\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n[ERROR] Failed on dataset {DATASET_NAME}: {str(e)}\")\n",
        "        results_summary.append({\n",
        "            \"Dataset\": DATASET_NAME,\n",
        "            \"Single_Acc\": None, \"Ens_Acc\": None, \"Time_Min\": None, \"Status\": \"Failed\"\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n",
        "print(\"FINAL SUMMARY REPORT\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*80)\n",
        "df_results = pd.DataFrame(results_summary)\n",
        "print(df_results.to_string(index=False, na_rep=\"N/A\"))\n",
        "# df_results.to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"benchmark_results_v2.csv\"), index=False)"
      ],
